# Skills · L · part 98

940 entries, looker to loop. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **looker**: Use for any Looker request — running a governed query or a saved Look, reading the LookML semantic model, creating Looks, dashboards or scheduled plans, or explaining why a Looker number disagrees with the Explore UI. Looker is the strongest platform for agentic analytics: it runs governed queries, exposes the full semantic model including an explicit join graph, and supports real content creation. Always set `query_timezone` explicitly and check whether the credential can see `sql`. Do not confuse Looker with Looker Studio, which has no data API at all. ([chimeaholu/agentic-gtm-analytics-skillpack](https://github.com/chimeaholu/agentic-gtm-analytics-skillpack/tree/HEAD/platforms/authoring/looker) · ★1)
